# Skills · S · part 288

835 entries, structural-variant-false-positive-classification to structured-output-design. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **structural-variant-false-positive-classification**: Use when a consensus SV caller (e.g. ConsensuSV-ONT on ONT reads) reports deletions that Truvari bench does not match to ground truth, and you need a per-FP mechanistic classification instead of a bare FP count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-false-positive-classification))
- **structural-variant-genotype-distance-stratification**: Use when crossing pairs of strains where variant call sets (SNPs, SVs, rearrangements, CNVs) and an offspring fitness measure (e.g. spore viability) are available and you must rank which variant classes explain reproductive isolation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-genotype-distance-stratification))
- **structural-variant-gold-set-assembly**: Use when a machine-learning SV caller needs labelled positive/negative training events, i.e. when real (non-simulated) gold-set call sets must be downloaded, interval-merged and converted into labelled feature vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-gold-set-assembly))
- **structural-variant-image-encoding**: Use when you have ONT/PacBio/Illumina read alignments (BAM) around candidate SV loci and need to convert them into fixed-size 50x50x3 image encodings for CNN-based variant quality filtering or cross-technology encoding comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-image-encoding))
- **structural-variant-image-representation**: Use when third-generation sequencing alignments (SAM/BAM, e.g. HG002 from GIAB AshkenazimTrio) must be converted into fixed-size images so that CIGAR-encoded deletion features can be fed to a multi-modal (image+sequence) deep learning SV caller. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-image-representation))
- **structural-variant-length-quantification**: Reference for deriving indel coordinates and lengths from gapped long-read alignments (SAM/BAM CIGAR strings from minimap2 or lra) when the variant is not represented as a split read and a consensus per-read median annotation is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-length-quantification))
- **structural-variant-overlap-matching**: Use when validating a genome-assembly error caller against known simulated error loci (e.g., SVs, indels, misjoins, inversions) and computing per-class recall, precision, and F1 with explicit overlap rules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-overlap-matching))
- **structural-variant-pathogenicity-prediction**: Use when you need to classify non-coding or coding SVs (inversions, translocations, deletions, duplications in hg19) as pathogenic vs non-pathogenic for congenital-abnormality phenotypes and recover the causative gene and long-range pathomechanism. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/structural-variant-pathogenicity-prediction))
- **structural-variant-phasing-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when long nanopore reads must be assigned to parental haplotypes via trio-phased heterozygous SNP anchors, or when SV calls need phasing from haplotype-assigned flanking reads and the result must be validated against trio-phased SNP ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-phasing-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **structural-variant-read-support-extraction**: Use when a candidate SV list (VCF from the consensus caller) must be converted into per-variant image tensors (50,50,3) as CNN input for high-quality variant filtering in an ONT long-read pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-read-support-extraction))
- **structural-variant-signature-graph-construction**: Use when reproducing or re-implementing dysgu's SV clustering: you have per-read SV signatures from split/discordant/supplementary alignments plus soft-clipped sequences and need to build a signature graph and quotient-graph partitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-signature-graph-construction))
- **structural-variant-simulation**: Use when benchmarking long-read mappers or SV callers for inversions and indels and you need simulated ground-truth SVs with noisy CLR/ONT reads at controlled coverage (10x-40x) against GRCh37 chr1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-simulation))
- **structural-variant-simulation-parameter-configuration**: Use when benchmarking a deletion SV caller needs known-truth simulated long-read data across homozygous/heterozygous groups and multiple coverage depths, requiring SURVIVOR parameter files plus PaSS PacBio read simulation and NGMLR alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-simulation-parameter-configuration))
- **structural-variant-size-stratification**: Use when Truvari bench/refine ga4gh VCFs from a long-read SV caller comparison are available and you need to know whether a caller's F1 advantage holds within specific SV length bins, not just genome-wide. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-size-stratification))
- **structural-variant-tool-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or reproducing comparisons of HGT breakpoint detection or local strain reconstruction methods on simulated shotgun metagenomic reads, where ground-truth breakpoints and transferred segments are known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-tool-benchmarking))
- **structural-variant-truth-set-benchmarking**: Use when you have merged SV or TE insertion VCF callsets and a curated truth set, and need precision/recall trade-off numbers to compare caller combinations or filtering criteria (strict vs lax). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-truth-set-benchmarking))
- **structural-variant-truth-set-recall-analysis**: Use when a reproduced nanopore variant-calling pipeline (VCFs from Sniffles/CuteSV/SVIM/NanoVar on minimap2/NGMLR/lra alignments) must be benchmarked against a published gold-standard SV set, split by SV type and size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-truth-set-recall-analysis))
- **structural-variant-vcf-merging**: Use when multiple per-caller structural variant VCFs exist for the same ONT sample and a single non-overlapping consensus SV candidate set is needed before consensus filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-vcf-merging))
- **structural-variant-vcf-parsing**: Use when you have a long-read SV caller's VCF (e.g., VolcanoSV VCF from Zenodo test data) and need to inspect, filter, or re-type records — specifically to recover DUP calls hidden among INS records. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/structural-variant-vcf-parsing))
